- **cross-national-design**: Designs multi-country survey experiments, covering case selection with a stated comparative logic, instrument localization and TRAPD translation, stimulus calibration, per-country power, and pooled versus per-country analysis with measurement-equivalence checks. Question wording goes to survey-design, reporting to methods-reporting, the study plan to pre-registration-writing. - **cross-role-technical-translator**: 将技术方案、架构文档、RFC、ADR、技术评审材料、事故复盘或工程师的口头说明，转译为产品、业务、管理者、运营、上下游团队与技术专家都能按各自职责理解和评估的分层信息。只要用户提到“让非技术同学看懂”“写给老板/产品/业务”“降低技术文档理解成本”“跨团队评审或对齐”“解释方案合理性、取舍、风险、影响”“把专业信息普世化”，或虽只说润色但材料将用于跨角色决策，就应使用本技能。它不是简单降维或删术语，而是在保持技术准确性的前提下补齐最小必要背景、显式区分事实与假设、连接技术机制与业务影响，并生成决策摘要、最小系统模型及其信息图、方案取舍、角色影响、风险证据和技术附录。当系统模型包含流程、层级、前后变化或三个以上相互关系时，优先调用 baoyu-infographic 生成可发布的信息图。纯粹面向同领域专家的实现细节编写、一般语法润色或代码实现任务不应触发，除非用户同时需要跨角色沟通。 ([17khba/agent-skills](https://github.com/17khba/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-role-technical-translator))

- **cross-sample-alignment-matrix-construction**: Use when you have already generated per-sample MS2 fingerprints (count vectors of MS2 peaks and neutral losses to the precursor) from matchms-processed spectra using spec2vec document representations, and you need to align these fingerprints into a single matrix to enable comparison across samples. - **cross-sample-cell-library-loss-quantification**: Use when multiplexed 10x Genomics droplet data sequenced on a patterned flow-cell instrument (HiSeq 4000/X/NovaSeq) needs per-sample assessment of how many cell calls are artefactual after swapped-molecule removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-cell-library-loss-quantification))
- **cross-sample-chip-signal-distribution-analysis**: Use when comparing ChIP-seq signal across ≥2 conditions with spike-in chromatin (e.g. drug titration series), especially when total-read normalization would mask a global occupancy reduction, and peak/background classification (MACS2 broad-peak BEDs) is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-chip-signal-distribution-analysis))
- **cross-sample-component-area-aggregation**: Use when a GC-MS hit table exported from vendor peak-detection software (e.g. Agilent Unknowns Analysis) contains multiple occurrences of the same tentative compound across samples and per-compound component areas must be collected and standardized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-sample-component-area-aggregation))
- **cross-sample-feature-alignment**: Use when you have run mass detection and chromatogram building independently on each LC-MS/MS sample and produced per-sample feature lists with m/z, retention time, and intensity values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-feature-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **cross-sample-feature-alignment**: Use when you have peak/feature tables per sample from multidimensional MS acquisitions and need one feature list matched across all study samples, QC pools, and blanks using simultaneous m/z, drift time, and retention time tolerances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-sample-feature-alignment))
- **cross-sample-feature-grouping**: Use when you have per-sample deconvolution results from untargeted GC×GC–MS data (e.g. GcDUO Module III output) and need to merge duplicate features into cross-sample consensus features with consensus spectra before annotation or integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-sample-feature-grouping))
- **cross-sample-feature-matching**: Use when you have detected feature tables from two or more LC-IMS-MS/MS samples and need to establish correspondence between features across samples to enable quantitative comparison, statistical analysis, or consensus feature calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-feature-matching) · 3 copies)
- **cross-sample-group-difference-estimation**: Use when running the TIC peak detection stage of AutoTuner and you must supply the Group difference value, the sole parameter estimated through a manual, non-automatable cross-sample comparison rather than statistical inference on raw data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-sample-group-difference-estimation))
- **cross-sample-hmp-contig-merge-evaluation**: Use when you have independently assembled contig FASTA files (any number of input Multi-FASTA sets) from related metagenome samples and want to merge them via OLC-based assembly into one improved contig set and evaluate contiguity gains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cross-sample-hmp-contig-merge-evaluation))
- **cross-sample-label-transfer**: Use when you have an annotated single-cell/nuclei RNA-seq dataset (e.g., .h5ad with 'class_label' in AnnData.obs) and need to predict cell-type labels on cells from a different donor or sample using SIMS label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-label-transfer))
- **cross-sample-metabolite-matching**: Use when after feature extraction (MS1 peak picking, MS2 recognition, or targeted list extraction) from multiple individual samples and before annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-metabolite-matching) · 2 copies)
- **cross-sample-network-coordinate-comparison**: Use when you already hold two denoised scGRNs (sparse dgCMatrix X and Y over the same gene set) and need commensurable per-gene coordinates to detect differential regulation between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-network-coordinate-comparison))
- **cross-sample-nmr-comparison**: Use when you have per-sample spin system feature tables (from combined TOCSY/HSQC peak picking) for a sample set and need pairwise sample similarity to drive network-based dereplication or bioactive metabolite prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-sample-nmr-comparison))
- **cross-sample-overlapping-slice-merging**: Use when building peak groups from LC-MS/MS lipidomics or metabolomics data (e.g., mzML files) where MS/MS scans must be aggregated across samples and assigned to finalized peak groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-sample-overlapping-slice-merging))

- **cross-service-contracts**: Designs and enforces wire contracts between repos and languages: one contract file per relationship, protobuf field discipline with buf breaking as a blocking gate, JSON-over-Kafka evolution, producer-made golden fixtures tested by PHP, Go, Rust and Node consumers, encrypted envelopes, pull-only control planes. Use when changing a shared message, schema or worker endpoint. Not for in-repo APIs. ([Rene-Roscher/skills](https://github.com/Rene-Roscher/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/go/skills/cross-service-contracts))
- **cross-service-deadline-propagation-audit**: Trace absolute deadlines through gateways, RPCs, queues and downstream services to find lost cancellation context or work that continues after callers give up. ([Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code](https://github.com/Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code/tree/HEAD/curated-skill-sources/release-testing-operations/cross-service-deadline-propagation-audit) · ★1 · 2 copies)
